<script>
    function fnAddProductToWishList(objSender) {
        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
        if (!isCustomerLoggedIn)
        {
            $(".login_new").show();
            $('.login_new_click').hide();
            $('.login_existing').hide();
            $(".forgot_password_view").hide();
            $("#myModalLabel").text('Sign Up');
            $("body.modal-open").css({"overflow": "visible", "padding-right": 0});
            $('#divLoginRegister').modal('show');
            $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
        } else
        {
            var intProductID = $(objSender).attr("pid");
            var intProductAttrID = $(objSender).attr("paid");
            if (intProductID && intProductAttrID)
            {
                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: strBaseURL + "myaccount/wishlist/addWishlistItem",
                    data: {product_id: intProductID, product_attributeid: intProductAttrID, ajax: '1',
                        pagename: 'minicart'},
                    beforeSend: function () {
                        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
                    },
                    success: function (data) {
                        $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
                        jsonResult = jQuery.parseJSON(data);
                        if (jsonResult.Status)
                        {
                            $('#divAlertContent p').html('Product added in Saved Items.');
                            $('#divTrendinAlert').modal('show');
                            window.setTimeout(function () {
                                $("#divTrendinAlert").modal("hide");
                                $('#menuCartItems').show();
                            }, 2000);
                            getMinicartDetails();
                        } else {
                            $('#divAlertContent p').html(jsonResult.Errors);
                            $('#divTrendinAlert').modal('show');
                            window.setTimeout(function () {
                                $("#divTrendinAlert").modal("hide");
                            }, 2000);
                            return false;
                        }
                    }

                });
            }
        }
    }
    function getMinicartDetails() {
        $.post(strBaseURL + "ajax/getMiniCartDetails", {type: 'Cart'}).done(function (res) {
            var parsedData = jQuery.parseJSON(res);
            $("#menuCartItems").html(parsedData.Cart);
            $("#wishlistitemsblck").html(parsedData.Wishlist);
        });
    }
    function fnDeleteProduct(size, qty) {
        objSender = $("#hdnDeleteProductFormName").val();
        if (objSender === '' || objSender === undefined)
            return false;
        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
        $.ajax({
            url: strBaseURL + 'cart/deleteProduct?isAjax=true',
            ajax: true,
            async: true,
            method: 'POST',
            data: $('form.' + objSender).serialize(),
            success: function (jsonResult)
            {
                $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
                jsonResult = jQuery.parseJSON(jsonResult);
                if (jsonResult.Status) {
                    $.post(strBaseURL + "ajax/getMiniCartDetails", {type: 'Cart'}).done(function (res) {
                        var parsedData = jQuery.parseJSON(res);
                        $("#menuCartItems").html(parsedData.Cart);
                        $("#wishlistitemsblck").html(parsedData.Wishlist);
                        $('#menuCartItems').show();
                    });
                } else {

                    $('#divAlertContent p').html(jsonResult.Errors);

<script>
    function fnAddProductToWishList(objSender) {
        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
        if (!isCustomerLoggedIn)
        {
            $(".login_new").show();
            $('.login_new_click').hide();
            $('.login_existing').hide();
            $(".forgot_password_view").hide();
            $("#myModalLabel").text('Sign Up');
            $("body.modal-open").css({"overflow": "visible", "padding-right": 0});
            $('#divLoginRegister').modal('show');
            $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
        } else
        {
            var intProductID = $(objSender).attr("pid");
            var intProductAttrID = $(objSender).attr("paid");
            if (intProductID && intProductAttrID)
            {
                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: strBaseURL + "myaccount/wishlist/addWishlistItem",
                    data: {product_id: intProductID, product_attributeid: intProductAttrID, ajax: '1',
                        pagename: 'minicart'},
                    beforeSend: function () {
                        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
                    },
                    success: function (data) {
                        $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
                        jsonResult = jQuery.parseJSON(data);
                        if (jsonResult.Status)
                        {
                            $('#divAlertContent p').html('Product added in Saved Items.');
                            $('#divTrendinAlert').modal('show');
                            window.setTimeout(function () {
                                $("#divTrendinAlert").modal("hide");
                                $('#menuCartItems').show();
                            }, 2000);
                            getMinicartDetails();
                        } else {
                            $('#divAlertContent p').html(jsonResult.Errors);
                            $('#divTrendinAlert').modal('show');
                            window.setTimeout(function () {
                                $("#divTrendinAlert").modal("hide");
                            }, 2000);
                            return false;
                        }
                    }

                });
            }
        }
    }
    function getMinicartDetails() {
        $.post(strBaseURL + "ajax/getMiniCartDetails", {type: 'Cart'}).done(function (res) {
            var parsedData = jQuery.parseJSON(res);
            $("#menuCartItems").html(parsedData.Cart);
            $("#wishlistitemsblck").html(parsedData.Wishlist);
        });
    }
    function fnDeleteProduct(size, qty) {
        objSender = $("#hdnDeleteProductFormName").val();
        if (objSender === '' || objSender === undefined)
            return false;
        $('#divGlobalLoader').show();
        $.ajax({
            url: strBaseURL + 'cart/deleteProduct?isAjax=true',
            ajax: true,
            async: true,
            method: 'POST',
            data: $('form.' + objSender).serialize(),
            success: function (jsonResult)
            {
                $('#divGlobalLoader').hide();
                jsonResult = jQuery.parseJSON(jsonResult);
                if (jsonResult.Status) {
                    $.post(strBaseURL + "ajax/getMiniCartDetails", {type: 'Cart'}).done(function (res) {
                        var parsedData = jQuery.parseJSON(res);
                        $("#menuCartItems").html(parsedData.Cart);
                        $("#wishlistitemsblck").html(parsedData.Wishlist);
                        $('#menuCartItems').show();
                    });
                } else {

                    $('#divAlertContent p').html(jsonResult.Errors);
                    $('#divTrendinAlert').modal('show');
                }
            },
            failure: function (jsonResult)
            {
                return false;
            }
        });
    }
</script>

Your Change</b></h2><p></p><p>Catering to the discerning and trendy consumer, Pantaloons is a power house of fashion and innovation. With a range of clothing from diverse sub brands that combine elegance and freshness, the unparalleled options are fashion forward and on trend. The collections cover a gamut of ready-to-wear western wear and<strong>ethnic wear for men, women and kids,</strong> in addition to a stunning range of<strong>accessories for womenand men</strong>. Pantaloons houses a repertoire of lifestyle brands that cater to all consumers across a range of multiple occasions. The family concept store has evolved into a fashion destination with a focus of delivering the <a href="https://www.pantaloons.com/"><strong>latest fashion</strong></a>.</p><p></p><h2><b>Pantaloons- A Repertoire of Brands</b></h2><p></p><p>Keeping the consumer’s fashion sensibilities and fashion in mind, Pantaloons has more than 200 brands on offer. Their reputed in-house brands come with their own noteworthy fan following and offers a wide range of brands for men, women and kids with categories including western wear, ethnic wear, formal wear, party wear, active wear and accessories making it a one stop destination for the entire family.</p><p></p><h2><b>Pantaloons- Fashionable Women's Wear</b></h2><p></p><p>The <a href="https://www.pantaloons.com/c/collections-455?page=1&amp;orderway=asc&amp;orderby=popular&amp;fp[]=Gender__fq:Women"><strong>Pantaloons women's wear</strong></a> collection caters to all occasions from work wear to festive wear. The exclusive ethnic wear brands include a mix-and-match range by Rangmanch, fusion wear from Akkriti and occasion wear from Trishaa. For the gym-bunnies, Ajile offers a range of stylish active wear, while Honey offers trendy casual wear. Perfect for elegant evening wear, Annabelle offers a range of dresses, skirts, tops and pants.</p><p></p><h2><b>Pantaloons- Smart Men's Wear</b></h2><p></p><p>The <a href="https://www.pantaloons.com/c/collections-455?page=1&amp;orderway=asc&amp;orderby=popular&amp;fp[]=Gender__fq:Men"><strong>Pantaloons men's wear</strong></a> collection caters to every need of the hour- from office wear to party wear. With edgy casuals from SF Jeans to preppy British sports-inspired clothing from Byford, the casual wear range is perfect for your off duty style. Fitness aficionados can pick trendy styles from active wear brand Ajile.</p><p></p><h2><b>Pantaloons- Cute Kids' Wear</b></h2><p></p>Not forgetting your little ones, Pantaloons also has an adorable collection that caters to toddlers and tweens alike.
